Act id: `legea-95-2006` (tags `legea-95-2006/<day>`; `python tools/on.py <day> legea-95-2006`).

## Decisions made by hand

- Legea nr. 95/2006: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocumentAfis/71139 as captured on 2026-03-09 (Internet Archive), the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 205 forms, 205 held.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the newest form held is that of 02.02.2026; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 372 of 28 April 2006 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/71139). Its art. 863 brings the titles into force in stages (3, 30 and 90 days after publication, the date of Romania's accession to the European Union, and later days); the first version carries the whole text from 1 May 2006, the day the first titles took effect.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the text republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 652 of 28 August 2015 under art. VI of Legea nr. 184/2015 (enacted 2 July 2015, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 490 of 3 July 2015), with renumbered articles, is read from its own page (DetaliiDocumentAfis/170974). Changes on that day that no note explains are credited to Legea nr. 184/2015, as the republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering, so many change that day.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the portal lists 205 forms (6 May 2006 to 2 February 2026, the republication among them). each is pinned in forms.json and in its version's source_url.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: art. 733 is left out from the form of 1 January 2007 with no note; the law's own art. 862 repeals it on the date of Romania's accession to the European Union (1 January 2007), read as published. Arts. 126-140 (title V) are left out from the form of 1 January 2009 (the portal prints a range title "Articolul 126 - 140" in some forms); OUG nr. 162/2008 art. 26 repeals title V, in force 1 January 2009 (its art. 25). Art. 202 is left out from the form of 10 June 2010; OUG nr. 48/2010 art. I pct. 42 repeals it, in force on publication, 10 June 2010. Art. 26^1 is left out from the form of 7 April 2017; Legea nr. 45/2017 (in force 3 days after publication, 7 April 2017) removed the point of OUG nr. 36/2016 that added it. Each repealing act was read as published.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: arts. 682, 684-687, 689 and 690 are left out from the form of 12 November 2009 with the portal's note that Legea nr. 329/2009 art. 68 lit. k) repealed them, "from the day the Government decisions setting up the new bodies take effect". The decision for the Școala Națională de Sănătate Publică și Management Sanitar, HOTĂRÂREA nr. 1.421/2009 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/114247), has no in-force clause of its own and was published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 848 of 8 December 2009, so the text stays through the form of 12 November 2009 and the repeal is dated 8 December 2009.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the forms of 1 January 2008 to 26 November 2008 print, after arts. 257 and 258, a second copy with the contribution rates that apply from a later day (the portal's note "Începând cu 1 iulie 2008, cotele ... vor fi următoarele"); the second copy is not the text in force and is left out.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the form of 13 October 2014 leaves out art. 11, with no note; the form of 4 November 2014 prints it again with the same text, so the earlier text is carried through the form of 13 October 2014. From 4 November 2014 the portal prints, after art. 11, a note quoted from art. III of OUG nr. 93/2008; it is a note, not the article's text, and is left out. Art. 819^16 (forms of 19 November 2012 to 27 December 2012, and 1 March 2013 to 23 May 2013) and art. 819^11 (8 June 2015) are left out and printed again unchanged, and are carried the same way.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: many changes between forms carry no portal note and are kept as printed, credited to no act: changes of diacritics or line breaks, and wording changes the notes of the forms do not attribute to a law (for example the global replacement of "Ministerul Sănătății Publice" by "Ministerul Sănătății" printed in the form of 5 January 2009).
- Legea nr. 95/2006: the pharmacists' oath in art. 559 (art. 569 in the text republished on 28 August 2015) ends with the line «Jur, pe onoare, în mod solemn și liber!"», which closes the quotation; that line is the last line of the article, not a marginal title of the next one, so it stays in it (flag keep_last_line, for those two articles only; corrected after publication, when the record had printed it at the start of art. 560, and of art. 570 from 28 August 2015).
- Legea nr. 95/2006: some forms print a wrong article number that repeats an earlier article's, between the two articles it sits between; Lexifina's code files it under the number in sequence: "Articolul 169" read as art. 619.
- Legea nr. 95/2006: changes a form prints before the in-force day its own notes give are dated by the notes (the earlier text stands until then): 2018-03-31 (art-875; printed in the form of 23.03.2018).
- Legea nr. 95/2006: articles a form leaves out whose repeal applies after that form keep their text until the repealing provision takes effect, and are shown repealed on that day: art. 682, left out from the form of 12.11.2009: repealed by Legea nr. 329/2009, in force for that provision on 08.12.2009 (its art. 68: the repeal takes effect when the Government decisions setting up the new bodies take effect; for the Școala Națională de Sănătate Publică și Management Sanitar that is HOTĂRÂREA nr. 1.421/2009 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/114247, capture 20260122141625), which has no in-force clause of its own: in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 848 of 8 December 2009), so its text stays through the form(s) of 12.11.2009; art. 684, left out from the form of 12.11.2009: repealed by Legea nr. 329/2009, in force for that provision on 08.12.2009 (its art. 68: the repeal takes effect when the Government decisions setting up the new bodies take effect; - Legea nr. 95/2006: changes whose notes give an in-force day between two forms the portal holds are dated by the note, with the later form's text for those articles: 2021-10-03 (art-875, art-935; form 31.12.2021); 2022-03-24 (art-701-1; form 26.03.2022); 2024-10-02 (art-221, art-236; form 11.10.2024).

## Constitutional Court findings not applied to this text

The Constitutional Court found the provisions below unconstitutional in a way the files here do not show as struck out: only some words inside a provision, a reading of it, a rule read from it, provisions read together with others, a provision amended or repealed before the decision took effect on it, a finding not matched automatically to one provision here, or a decision given before the 2003 revision of the Constitution. The text here is unchanged; read the decision. `data/court-notes.csv` lists every such finding.

- **[Decizia nr. 1394/2010](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2010/1394/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 863 din 23 decembrie 2010 · art. 257 alin. (2) lit. f) · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 257 alin. (2) lit. f) teza finală din Legea nr. 95/2006 sunt neconstituţionale în măsura în care se interpretează că valoarea contribuţiei minime la fondul de asigurări sociale de sănătate, datorată de persoanele care realizează venituri din cedarea folosinţei bunurilor, venituri din dividende şi dobânzi, venituri din drepturi de proprietate intelectuală realizate în mod individual şi/sau într-o formă de asociere şi alte venituri care se supun impozitului pe venit, nu poate fi mai mică decât cuantumul unui salariu de bază minim brut pe ţară, lunar.

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2010-12-23) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 335/2011](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2011/335/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 355 din 23 mai 2011 · art. 257 alin. (2) lit. f) · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 257 alin. (2) lit. f) teza finală din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, aşa cum au fost modificate de art. I pct. 4 din Ordonanţa de urgenţă a Guvernului nr. 107/2010 pentru modificarea şi completarea Legii nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, sunt neconstituţionale în măsura în care se interpretează că valoarea contribuţiei minime la fondul de asigurări sociale de sănătate, datorată de persoanele care realizează venituri din cedarea folosinţei bunurilor, venituri din dividende şi dobânzi, venituri din drepturi de proprietate intelectuală realizate în mod individual şi/sau într-o formă de asociere şi alte venituri care se supun impozitului pe venit, nu poate fi mai mică decât cuantumul unui salariu de bază minim brut pe ţară, lunar.

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2011-05-23) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 223/2012](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2012/223/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 256 din 18 aprilie 2012 · art. 259 alin. (2) · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > Constată că dispoziţiile art. 259 alin. (2) din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, care au format obiectul excepţiei de neconstituţionalitate \[…\]

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2012-04-18) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 224/2012](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2012/224/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 256 din 18 aprilie 2012 · art. 259 alin. (2) · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > Constată că dispoziţiile art. 259 alin. (2) din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, care au format obiectul excepţiei de neconstituţionalitate \[…\]

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2012-04-18) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 164/2013](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2013/164/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 296 din 23 mai 2013 · art. 257 alin. (2) lit. e) · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 257 alin. (2) lit. e) din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, în redactarea de la intrarea în vigoare a acestora şi până la modificările ce le-au fost aduse prin Ordonanţa de urgenţă a Guvernului nr. 93/2008 pentru modificarea şi completarea Legii nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătăţii, sunt neconstituţionale în măsura în care obligă persoanele ce datorează contribuţia de asigurări sociale de sănătate asupra veniturilor din pensii la plata acestei contribuţii cumulat cu cea datorată asupra veniturilor obţinute din cedarea folosinţei bunurilor, din dividende şi dobânzi, din drepturi de proprietate intelectuală realizate în mod individual şi/sau într-o formă de asociere şi altor venituri care se supun impozitului pe venit.

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2013-05-23) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 498/2018](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2018/498/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 650 din 26 iulie 2018 · art. 30 alin. (2); art. 280 alin. (2) · only some words or a sentence inside the provision

  > constată că dispozițiile art. 30 alin. (2) și (3), precum și sintagma „sistemul dosarului electronic de sănătate al pacientului“ din cuprinsul art. 280 alin. (2) din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătății sunt neconstituționale.

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2018-07-26) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2018-09-09, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 818/2019](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2019/818/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 561 din 29 iunie 2020 · art. 320 alin. (1) · only some words or a sentence inside the provision

  > constată că sintagma „precum și daune sănătății propriei persoane“ din cuprinsul art. 320 alin. (1) din Legea nr. 95/2006 privind reforma în domeniul sănătății este neconstituțională.

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2020-06-29) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2020-08-13, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.
